| **N35 / P / A — substitutions while typing** | Opt-in ellipsis/dash/arrow/comparison typography under stated conditions. | [Typing][r1-o-typing] pairs/inserts without these replacements. [TextOp/tables][r1-o-transform-model] supply explicit batch transforms. Native macOS substitutions were not tested: this concerns an **Orlo-configured** feature. | FeatherPad [Automatic String Replacement help][r1-fp-help]/[keyPressEvent][r1-fp-edit], gated by autoReplace/language/key conditions. **Defer** or opt-in prose only; no automatic punctuation change by default. |
| **N36 / P / A — list-to-grid** | Reshape lines into N columns, horizontal/vertical distribution, separator/fill. | [CSV/column catalog][r1-o-entries]/[ColumnOp][r1-o-csv] move/select fields, number and pad, not redistribute lines into a grid. Shell filters can do it. | Bluefish [features](https://bluefish.openoffice.nl/features.html)/[menu][r1-bf-menu]/[convert_to_columns_backend][r1-bf-text]. **Defer/optional** niche utility; general CSV/columns already exist. |
| **N37 / P / +C — conditional macro repetition** | Stop at stable text or the last line, beyond a fixed repeat count. | [repeat_macro/play_macro][r1-o-custom] run a positive fixed count with bounds, no caret/text predicate. Existing task/filter scripting is an alternative. | CudaText [Macros docs][r1-cu-plugins]/[upstream plugin snapshot Command.run](https://github.com/kvichans/cuda_macros/blob/84c37ae593cfb5492f5f348c96d4d2eb6ea0ddbf/cd_macros.py) checks snapshots/last line and time/cancel. **Optional plugin snapshot**, not core/bundled or a release-matched installed version. **Defer/optional**, requiring bounds/cancellation. |
| **N38 / P / +H — per-selection shell filters** | Transform each selection independently or retain ranges by command exit status. | [FilterSnapshot::new](../../crates/editor/src/commands/filter.rs) explicitly requires **one** selection; a caret filters the document. [RunShellCommand][r1-o-custom] outputs a scratch view, not independent selected inputs. | Helix [keymap/shell docs][r3-h-keymap]/[pipe, keep_pipe handlers][r3-h-shell] iterate selections; output insertion has separate semantics. **Optional** existing-shell refinement, distinct from interactive terminal sending T07. |

### Terminal, Git and lightweight language tools

| ID / state / novelty | Precise behavior and benefit | Orlo implementation check | Peer documentation and implementation; fit |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **N39 / P / B — synchronize terminal cwd** | Change an **already running** terminal to the active document/project directory. | [show_terminal][r1-o-terminal] picks file/project/home cwd only on construction, then reuses the entity. Show/close actions do not synchronize cwd. OSC7 T02 tracks output cwd in the opposite direction. | Kate [Konsole docs](https://docs.kde.org/stable_kf6/en/kate/kate/kate-application-plugin-konsole.html)/[slotManualSync, cd][r1-ka-terminal] quote commands and guard foreground processes/interpreters; **optional app plugin, not KWrite**. **Optional**; avoid sending cd into foreground applications. |
| **N40 / P / +H — type/implementation destinations** | Go to type definition or interface implementations. | [Feature dispatch](../../crates/ui/src/views/workspace/language.rs) requests definition/references/hover/rename/format/actions, not `textDocument/typeDefinition` or `textDocument/implementation`. Ordinary navigation exists. | Helix [keymap][r3-h-keymap]/[handlers][r3-h-lsp-nav]/[capability-gated requests][r3-h-lsp-client]. **Optional** small LSP extension with existing location UI/stale checks. |
| **N41 / P / A — inline diagnostic messages** | Optionally show messages at line ends without opening a picker. | [Renderer][r1-o-element] draws underline/gutter; [lsp_diagnostics](../../crates/ui/src/views/workspace/language.rs) shows messages across **all open tabs** in a picker, not after source text. | Fresh [editing guide][r3-fresh-edit-doc]/[per-line map][r3-fresh-diag-model]/[renderer][r3-fresh-diag], default off; Helix [settings][r3-h-editor] corroborate. **Optional/off by default**, not missing diagnostics or missing open-tab coverage. |
| **N42 / P / +H — language setup health** | Report missing server executables/runtime/query data together. | [Language UI](../../crates/ui/src/views/workspace/language.rs) already has status/restart/errors; [manifest validation](../../crates/syntax/src/languages/manifest.rs) is not a combined setup probe/report. | Helix [install docs][r3-h-install]/[language config][r3-h-language]/[health implementation][r3-h-health]. **Optional/defer**, especially until L05 needs it; exclude DAP health probes. |
| **N43 / P / B — local Git tags** | List/create/delete lightweight or annotated tags. | [Git exports](../../crates/git/src/lib.rs)/[branches](../../crates/git/src/branches/mod.rs) have no tag lifecycle API/UI. Linked/nested repositories already work; no universal submodule absence claim. | Athas [guide][r3-athas-doc]/[tag UI][r3-athas-tag-ui]/[backend][r3-athas-tags] expose local tags; backend signing is not ordinary-dialog proof. Nova [reference/submodule docs](https://help.nova.app/git/sidebar/) docs-only. **Optional/defer**, not a merge/rebase/hosting-suite requirement. |

<a id="second-pass-priorities"></a>

### Second-pass priorities

Keep section 4's initial practical priorities. These additions are choices for small releases, not a request to implement all 43 groups.

- **Worth adding:** URI/HTML text conversion (N01–N02), subword actions (N04), Save a Copy As (N22), focused syntax-node expansion and language-aware indentation (N16–N17) where the existing backend supports a simple implementation.
- **Optional:** literal escaping, selection complement/filtering, change-surround, prior-hit-line search, reversible filter views, bookmark picker, document path activation, viewer toggle, natural sort, field alignment, snippet choices/date-time values, per-selection shell filtering, terminal cwd sync and the two extra LSP destinations. Inline messages remain opt-in.
- **Defer:** streamed hex viewing, branched undo, broad AST queries, changing Tab behavior, micromap, typing substitutions, elaborate mark stacks, conditional macros, sticky explorer rows and list-grid tools. Persistent undo needs a storage/recovery specification. Extra Git tags and setup-health UI can wait for demand.

The earlier implementation prompt selected 27 first-pass items. **This review does not automatically add the N-items to that prompt or authorize product-code changes.** A later prompt should name chosen IDs, exact behavior and acceptance checks.

Fixed rulers and current-line gutter emphasis already exist. | **+H, optional/defer display variants**, recorded separately from the 43 workflow groups; no modal interpreter or new default required. |

### Corrections: existing Orlo behavior

| Under-described behavior | Inspected evidence and correction |
| --- | --- |
| Bracket/quote selection wrapping | [type_text][r1-o-typing] wraps nonempty selections; empty-pair backspace exists. N07 only changes/deletes an existing nonempty surround. |
| Dotted indentation guides | [guide_levels/painting][r1-o-element] carry indentation across blank lines. Not missing. |
| CR output and several Unicode-space marks | [Menus](../../crates/ui/src/views/workspace/menus.rs) offer LF/CRLF/Mac OS 9 CR; [white/Mark::Other][r1-o-element] mark NBSP/en/em/ideographic and other spaces when enabled. E03 concerns configurable warning policy/broader codepoints; N25 concerns mixed endings. |
| Retained search text/refresh | [results_view/run_search][r1-o-find-ui] append successive runs and refresh the latest structured set. N13 concerns independent models, N12 hit-line scope. |
| Caret alignment/numbering/transforms | [pad_to_column](../../crates/editor/src/commands/column_editing.rs), [Sequence](../../crates/editor/src/editing/columns.rs), eight case modes, selection permutation, matching-line and CSV tools already cover many peer examples. N09/N10/N30 are narrower. |
| Open-tab diagnostics/session bookmarks | [lsp_diagnostics](../../crates/ui/src/views/workspace/language.rs) iterates all open tabs; [sessions][r1-o-session] retain bookmarks. N41 is inline display, N20 cross-buffer discovery. |
| Minimap/hot exit/backups/inline folds/Git/PTY | O17–O35 remain implemented. A TUI is not an embedded terminal, recovery is not persistent undo and parsing alone is not a fold UI. |

### Excluded additions

Keep section 5's exclusions. The new peers preserve the same product boundary:

- Helix [DAP/debug commands][r3-h-commands-doc] belong in the separate IDE. Its modal interpreter remains E09/deferred; individual text actions can be used independently.
- CudaText [Python Console/API, plugins and HTML features][r1-cu-wiki] include evaluation, images, web-authoring helpers, color pickers, FTP and compile/run integrations. Console is not a PTY. Do not add Python or a marketplace to obtain small text tools.
- Acme command tags/9P, Leo clones/literate files, Gobby collaboration, Brackets browser Quick Edit and Kate SQL/compiler/debug plugins remain excluded.
- BeanShell/JavaScript/Python computed replacements are real peer features, but embedded runtimes remain deferred. Orlo's explicit shell filters offer an alternative.

### All-50 second-pass coverage ledger

Every row records fresh official inventory/command/source inspection. **C** = already catalogued or Orlo-supported; **D** = excluded/deferred. A no-new-gap outcome is explicit. Versions/platforms/full pins remain in the registry and P01–P50.

### Second-pass verification and remaining uncertainty

- Research/source access dates: **2026-10-29–30**, same Orlo commit. All 50 products have registry rows, detailed profiles and a second-pass ledger entry. Peer binaries were not executed.
- [Native menus](../../crates/ui/src/views/workspace/menus.rs), [command catalog][r1-o-entries], [action registrations][r1-o-actions], responsible models/handlers and relevant existing tests were inspected. An internal helper/dependency alone was not treated as user support.
- Two existing UI tests were actually run: `cargo test -p orlo-ui --locked guide_levels_count_the_indent_and_carry_over_blank_lines` and `cargo test -p orlo-ui --locked all_marks_every_space_tab_other_space_and_the_line_end`. **Each passed 1 test, 0 failed, 963 filtered.** They resolve guide/whitespace inventory corrections, not all N-items. Section 8 records the earlier folding test. No full suite was run for this documentation-only task.
- CudaText's wiki challenge was handled using the **upstream release's own wiki mirror**. Current Windows/Linux and older macOS/BSD/Solaris artifacts stay distinct. ATSynEdit and optional Macros were separately pinned; exact bundled versions/default enablement are not assumed.
- Closed sources, unsupported historical apps, old manuals, optional-plugin requirements, service availability, macOS Services/Character Viewer, accessibility/BiDi, large-file performance and OS parity remain qualified. Source inspection does not establish runtime behavior under those conditions.
- Completed-artifact checks confirmed **50 registry rows/profiles/dossier anchors and 50 second-pass ledger entries**, **37 Orlo inventory groups**, the original **33 gap rows** and **43 additional behavior groups (4 M, 39 P)**. All **366 reference definitions** resolve without duplicates; all **158 local targets**, internal dossier/section links, table widths, code fences and whitespace checks pass. All **320 distinct commit-pinned GitHub source/document targets currently cited** reached successfully (including reused initial-pass checks); **36 newly introduced non-GitHub targets** reached. A cached legacy Bluefish manual was not directly accessible (404), so current-release claims rely on its live feature inventory and inspected release sources instead. Existing SourceForge restrictions remain recorded. HTTP reachability is not a behavioral test.
